Mathlib.Topology.Algebra.Module.ContinuousLinearMap.Basic

ContinuousLinearMapstructureMathlib.Topology.Algebra.Module.ContinuousLinearMap.Basic, type
Wording
continuous linear map of M and x5 (from the name)
English
Let $x_{2}$ be a ring homomorphism of $R$ and $S$. Let $M$ and $x_{5}$ be sets. Then the continuous linear maps of $M$ and $x_{5}$ are a basic type.
Formula
$x_{2} \in \mathrm{RingHom} (R , S), M , x_{5} \in \mathrm{Set} \vdash \mathrm{ContinuousLinearMap} (x_{2} , M , x_{5})\ \mathsf{set}$
French
Soit $x_{2}$ un homomorphisme d'anneaux de $R$ et $S$. Soient $M$ et $x_{5}$ des ensembles. Alors les applications linéaires continues de $M$ et $x_{5}$ sont un type de base.
ContinuousLinearMap.coeLMdefMathlib.Topology.Algebra.Module.ContinuousLinearMap.Basic, function or constant
Wording
no wording
English
Let $S$ be a set. Then $\operatorname{coeLM}$ applied to $S$ is a linear map of the set of continuous linear maps of $M$ and $x_{5}$ and the set of linear maps of $M$ and $x_{5}$.
Formula
$S \in \mathrm{Set} \vdash \operatorname{coeLM} (S)\in \mathrm{LinearMap} (\operatorname{id} (S), \mathrm{ContinuousLinearMap} (\operatorname{id} (R), M , x_{5}), \mathrm{LinearMap} (\operatorname{id} (R), M , x_{5}))$
French
Soit $S$ un ensemble. Alors $\operatorname{coeLM}$ appliqué à $S$ est une application linéaire de l'ensemble d'applications linéaires continues de $M$ et $x_{5}$ et l'ensemble d'applications linéaires de $M$ et $x_{5}$.

ContinuousLinearMap.compdefMathlib.Topology.Algebra.Module.ContinuousLinearMap.Basic, function or constant
Wording
the composition of g and f (from the name)
English
Let $g$ be a continuous linear map of $x_{12}$ and $x_{15}$. Let $f$ be a continuous linear map of $x_{9}$ and $x_{12}$. Then the composition of $g$ and $f$ is a continuous linear map of $x_{9}$ and $x_{15}$.
Formula
$g \in \mathrm{ContinuousLinearMap} (x_{7} , x_{12} , x_{15}), f \in \mathrm{ContinuousLinearMap} (x_{6} , x_{9} , x_{12})\vdash \operatorname{comp} (g , f)\in \mathrm{ContinuousLinearMap} (x_{8} , x_{9} , x_{15})$
French
Soit $g$ une application linéaire continue de $x_{12}$ et $x_{15}$. Soit $f$ une application linéaire continue de $x_{9}$ et $x_{12}$. Alors la composition de $g$ et $f$ est une application linéaire continue de $x_{9}$ et $x_{15}$.
ContinuousLinearMap.copydefMathlib.Topology.Algebra.Module.ContinuousLinearMap.Basic, function or constant
Wording
the copy of f and h (from the name)
English
Let $f$ be a continuous linear map of $x_{5}$ and $x_{8}$. Let $\mathrm{f'}$ be a function from elements of $x_{5}$ to elements of $x_{8}$ such that $\mathrm{f'}$ is equal to $\operatorname{coe}$ applied to $f$. Then the copy of $f$ and $\mathrm{f'}$ is a continuous linear map of $x_{5}$ and $x_{8}$.
Formula
$f \in \mathrm{ContinuousLinearMap} (x_{4} , x_{5} , x_{8}), \mathrm{f'} \in x_{5} \rightarrow x_{8} , \EqtMACRoJ {\mathrm{f'}}{\operatorname{coe} (f)}\vdash \operatorname{copy} (f , \mathrm{f'} , h)\in \mathrm{ContinuousLinearMap} (x_{4} , x_{5} , x_{8})$
French
Soit $f$ une application linéaire continue de $x_{5}$ et $x_{8}$. Soit $\mathrm{f'}$ une fonction des éléments de $x_{5}$ à des éléments de $x_{8}$ telle que $\mathrm{f'}$ soit égal à $\operatorname{coe}$ appliqué à $f$. Alors le copy de $f$ et $\mathrm{f'}$ est une application linéaire continue de $x_{5}$ et $x_{8}$.
ContinuousLinearMap.homeomorphOfUnitdefMathlib.Topology.Algebra.Module.ContinuousLinearMap.Basic, function or constant
Wording
the homeomorphism of T (from the name)
English
Let $T$ be a unit of the set of continuous linear maps of $x_{2}$ and $x_{2}$. Then the homeomorphism of $T$ is a homeomorphism of $x_{2}$ and $x_{2}$.
Formula
$T \in \UnitsMACRoAX {\mathrm{ContinuousLinearMap} (\operatorname{id} (x), x_{2} , x_{2})}\vdash \operatorname{homeomorphOfUnit} (T)\in \mathrm{Homeomorph} (x_{2} , x_{2})$
French
Soit $T$ une unité de l'ensemble d'applications linéaires continues de $x_{2}$ et $x_{2}$. Alors l'homéomorphisme de $T$ est un homéomorphisme de $x_{2}$ et $x_{2}$.
ContinuousLinearMap.iddefMathlib.Topology.Algebra.Module.ContinuousLinearMap.Basic, function or constant
Wording
the identity map of x and x2 (from the docstring)
English
Let $x$ and $x_{2}$ be sets. Then the identity map of $x$ and $x_{2}$ is a continuous linear map of $x_{2}$ and $x_{2}$ defined as $\operatorname{mk}$ applied to the identity and $\mathrm{continuous\_id}$.
Formula
$x , x_{2} \in \mathrm{Set} \vdash \operatorname{id} (x , x_{2}):= \operatorname{mk} (\operatorname{id} , \mathrm{continuous\_id})\in \mathrm{ContinuousLinearMap} (\operatorname{id} (x), x_{2} , x_{2})$
French
Soient $x$ et $x_{2}$ des ensembles. Alors l'application d'identités de $x$ et $x_{2}$ est une application linéaire continue de $x_{2}$ et $x_{2}$ défini comme $\operatorname{mk}$ appliqué à l'identité et $\mathrm{continuous\_id}$.
ContinuousLinearMap.lcompdefMathlib.Topology.Algebra.Module.ContinuousLinearMap.Basic, function or constant
Wording
no wording
English
Let $W$ be a set. Let $f$ be a continuous linear map of $U$ and $V$. Then $\operatorname{lcomp}$ applied to $W$ and $f$ is a linear map of the set of continuous linear maps of $V$ and $W$ and the set of continuous linear maps of $U$ and $W$.
Formula
$W \in \mathrm{Set} , f \in \mathrm{ContinuousLinearMap} (\operatorname{id} (R), U , V)\vdash \operatorname{lcomp} (W , f)\in \mathrm{LinearMap} (\operatorname{id} (R), \mathrm{ContinuousLinearMap} (\operatorname{id} (R), V , W), \mathrm{ContinuousLinearMap} (\operatorname{id} (R), U , W))$
French
Soit $W$ un ensemble. Soit $f$ une application linéaire continue de $U$ et $V$. Alors $\operatorname{lcomp}$ appliqué à $W$ et $f$ est une application linéaire de l'ensemble d'applications linéaires continues de $V$ et $W$ et l'ensemble d'applications linéaires continues de $U$ et $W$.

Wording

English
Let $R$, $U$, $V$ and $W$ be sets. Then $\operatorname{llcomp}$ applied to $R$, $U$, $V$ and $W$ is a linear map of the set of continuous linear maps of $U$ and $V$ and the set of linear maps of the set of continuous linear maps of $V$ and $W$ and the set of continuous linear maps of $U$ and $W$.

Alors $\operatorname{llcomp}$ appliqué à $R$, $U$, $V$ et $W$ est une application linéaire de l'ensemble d'applications linéaires continues de $U$ et $V$ et l'ensemble d'applications linéaires de l'ensemble d'applications linéaires continues de $V$ et $W$ et l'ensemble d'applications linéaires continues de $U$ et $W$.

English
Let $R$, $S$ and $M$ be sets. Then $\operatorname{toSpanSingletonLE}$ applied to $R$, $S$ and $M$ is a linear equivalence of $M$ and the set of continuous linear maps of $R$ and $M$.
Formula
$R , S , M \in \mathrm{Set} \vdash \operatorname{toSpanSingletonLE} (R , S , M)\in \mathrm{LinearEquiv} (\operatorname{id} (S), M , \mathrm{ContinuousLinearMap} (\operatorname{id} (R), R , M))$

ContinuousLinearMapClassdefMathlib.Topology.Algebra.Module.ContinuousLinearMap.Basic, predicate
Wording
no wording
English
Let $F$ be a set. Let $R$, $M$ and $x_{3}$ be elements of $\mathrm{outParam}$ applied to the set of sets. Then $\mathrm{ContinuousLinearMapClass}$ holds for $F$, $R$, $M$ and $x_{3}$, if $\mathrm{ContinuousSemilinearMapClass}$ holds for $F$, the identity ring homomorphism of $R$, $M$ and $x_{3}$.
Formula
$F \in \mathrm{Set} , R , M , x_{3} \in \mathrm{outParam} (\mathrm{Set})\vdash \mathrm{ContinuousLinearMapClass} (F , R , M , x_{3}):\Leftrightarrow \mathrm{ContinuousSemilinearMapClass} (F , \operatorname{id} (R), M , x_{3})$
French
Soit $F$ un ensemble. Soient $R$, $M$ et $x_{3}$ des éléments de $\mathrm{outParam}$ appliqué à l'ensemble d'ensembles. Alors $\mathrm{ContinuousLinearMapClass}$ tient pour $F$, $R$, $M$ et $x_{3}$ si $\mathrm{ContinuousSemilinearMapClass}$ tient pour $F$, l'homomorphisme d'anneaux d'identités de $R$, $M$ et $x_{3}$.
ContinuousSemilinearMapClassclassMathlib.Topology.Algebra.Module.ContinuousLinearMap.Basic, predicate
Wording
no wording
English
Let $F$ be a set. Let $x_{2}$ be an element of $\mathrm{outParam}$ applied to the set of ring homomorphisms of $R$ and $S$. Let $M$ and $x_{5}$ be elements of $\mathrm{outParam}$ applied to the set of sets. Then we can say that $\mathrm{ContinuousSemilinearMapClass}$ holds for $F$, $x_{2}$, $M$ and $x_{5}$.
Formula
$F \in \mathrm{Set} , x_{2} \in \mathrm{outParam} (\mathrm{RingHom} (R , S)), M , x_{5} \in \mathrm{outParam} (\mathrm{Set})\vdash \mathrm{ContinuousSemilinearMapClass} (F , x_{2} , M , x_{5})\ \mathsf{prop}$
French
Soit $F$ un ensemble. Soit $x_{2}$ un élément de $\mathrm{outParam}$ appliqué à l'ensemble d'homomorphismes d'anneaux de $R$ et $S$. Soient $M$ et $x_{5}$ des éléments de $\mathrm{outParam}$ appliqué à l'ensemble d'ensembles. Alors nous pouvons dire que $\mathrm{ContinuousSemilinearMapClass}$ tient pour $F$, $x_{2}$, $M$ et $x_{5}$.
StrongDualdefMathlib.Topology.Algebra.Module.ContinuousLinearMap.Basic, type
Wording
strong dual of R and M (from the name)
English
Let $R$ and $M$ be sets. Then a strong dual of $R$ and $M$ is an element of the function that maps $R$ and $M$ to the set of continuous linear maps of $M$ and $R$.
Formula
$R , M \in \mathrm{Set} \vdash \mathrm{StrongDual} (R , M):= \lambda R , M . \mathrm{ContinuousLinearMap} (\operatorname{id} (R), M , R)$
French
Soient $R$ et $M$ des ensembles. Alors un dual fort de $R$ et $M$ est un élément de la fonction qui envoie $R$ et $M$ à l'ensemble d'applications linéaires continues de $M$ et $R$.
topDualPairingdefMathlib.Topology.Algebra.Module.ContinuousLinearMap.Basic, function or constant
Wording
the canonical pairing of x and E (from the docstring)
English
Let $x$ and $E$ be sets. Then the canonical pairing of $x$ and $E$ is a linear map of the set of continuous linear maps of $E$ and $x$ and the set of linear maps of $E$ and $x$ defined as $\operatorname{coeLM}$ applied to $x$.
Formula
$x , E \in \mathrm{Set} \vdash \mathrm{topDualPairing} (x , E):= \operatorname{coeLM} (x)\in \mathrm{LinearMap} (\operatorname{id} (x), \mathrm{ContinuousLinearMap} (\operatorname{id} (x), E , x), \mathrm{LinearMap} (\operatorname{id} (x), E , x))$
French
Soient $x$ et $E$ des ensembles. Alors le pairing canonique de $x$ et $E$ est une application linéaire de l'ensemble d'applications linéaires continues de $E$ et $x$ et l'ensemble d'applications linéaires de $E$ et $x$ défini comme $\operatorname{coeLM}$ appliqué à $x$.

ContinuousLinearMap.extenddefMathlib.Topology.Algebra.Module.ContinuousLinearMap.Extend, function or constant
Wording
the extension of f and e (from the name, with words of the lexicon)
English
Let $f$ be a continuous linear map of $E$ and $F$. Let $e$ be a continuous linear map of $E$ and $x_{2}$. Then the extension of $f$ and $e$ is a continuous linear map of $x_{2}$ and $F$.
Formula
$f \in \mathrm{ContinuousLinearMap} (x_{14} , E , F), e \in \mathrm{ContinuousLinearMap} (\operatorname{id} (x), E , x_{2})\vdash \operatorname{extend} (f , e)\in \mathrm{ContinuousLinearMap} (x_{14} , x_{2} , F)$
French
Soit $f$ une application linéaire continue de $E$ et $F$. Soit $e$ une application linéaire continue de $E$ et $x_{2}$. Alors l'extension de $f$ et $e$ est une application linéaire continue de $x_{2}$ et $F$.

Mathlib.Topology.Algebra.Module.ContinuousLinearMap.Invertible

ContinuousLinearMap.IsInvertibledefMathlib.Topology.Algebra.Module.ContinuousLinearMap.Invertible, predicate
Wording
f is invertible (from the docstring)
English
Let $f$ be a continuous linear map of $M$ and $x$. Then $f$ is invertible, if there exists a continuous linear equivalence $A$ of $M$ and $x$, such that the continuous linear map of $A$ is equal to $f$.
Formula
$f \in \mathrm{ContinuousLinearMap} (\operatorname{id} (R), M , x)\vdash \operatorname{IsInvertible} (f):\Leftrightarrow \exists A \in \mathrm{ContinuousLinearEquiv} (\operatorname{id} (R), M , x). \EqtMACRoJ {\operatorname{toContinuousLinearMap} (A)}{f}$
French
Soit $f$ une application linéaire continue de $M$ et $x$. Alors $f$ est inversible s'il existe une équivalence linéaire continue $A$ de $M$ et $x$ tel que l'application linéaire continue de $A$ soit égale à $f$.
ContinuousLinearMap.inversedefMathlib.Topology.Algebra.Module.ContinuousLinearMap.Invertible, function or constant
Wording
the inverse of x8 (from the name)
English
Let $x_{8}$ be a continuous linear map of $M$ and $x$. Then the inverse of $x_{8}$ is a continuous linear map of $x$ and $M$ defined as $\mathrm{dite}$ applied to the proposition that $x_{8}$ is invertible, the function that maps $h$ to the continuous linear map of the inverse of $\operatorname{choose}$ applied to $h$ and the function that maps $h_{1}$ to $0$.
Formula
$x_{8} \in \mathrm{ContinuousLinearMap} (\operatorname{id} (R), M , x)\vdash \operatorname{inverse} (x_{8}):= \mathrm{dite} (\operatorname{IsInvertible} (x_{8}), \lambda h . \operatorname{toContinuousLinearMap} (\operatorname{symm} (\operatorname{choose} (h))), \lambda h_{1} . 0)\in \mathrm{ContinuousLinearMap} (\operatorname{id} (R), x , M)$
French
Soit $x_{8}$ une application linéaire continue de $M$ et $x$. Alors l'inverse de $x_{8}$ est une application linéaire continue de $x$ et $M$ défini comme $\mathrm{dite}$ appliqué à la proposition que $x_{8}$ est inversible, la fonction qui envoie $h$ à l'application linéaire continue de l'inverse de $\operatorname{choose}$ appliqué à $h$ et la fonction qui envoie $h_{1}$ à $0$.

ContinuousLinearMap.coproddefMathlib.Topology.Algebra.Module.ContinuousLinearMap.PiProd, function or constant
Wording
the coproduct of x12 and x13 (from the name)
English
Let $x_{12}$ be a continuous linear map of $x$ and $M$. Let $x_{13}$ be a continuous linear map of $x_{1}$ and $M$. Then the coproduct of $x_{12}$ and $x_{13}$ is a continuous linear map of $\mathrm{Prod}$ applied to $x$ and $x_{1}$ and $M$.
Formula
$x_{12} \in \mathrm{ContinuousLinearMap} (\operatorname{id} (R), x , M), x_{13} \in \mathrm{ContinuousLinearMap} (\operatorname{id} (R), x_{1} , M)\vdash \operatorname{coprod} (x_{12} , x_{13})\in \mathrm{ContinuousLinearMap} (\operatorname{id} (R), \mathrm{Prod} (x , x_{1}), M)$
French
Soit $x_{12}$ une application linéaire continue de $x$ et $M$. Soit $x_{13}$ une application linéaire continue de $x_{1}$ et $M$. Alors le coproduct de $x_{12}$ et $x_{13}$ est une application linéaire continue de $\mathrm{Prod}$ appliqué à $x$ et $x_{1}$ et $M$.
ContinuousLinearMap.coprodEquivdefMathlib.Topology.Algebra.Module.ContinuousLinearMap.PiProd, function or constant
Wording
the coproduct equivalence (from the name)
English
The coproduct equivalence is a linear equivalence of $\mathrm{Prod}$ applied to the set of continuous linear maps of $x$ and $M$ and the set of continuous linear maps of $x_{1}$ and $M$ and the set of continuous linear maps of $\mathrm{Prod}$ applied to $x$ and $x_{1}$ and $M$.
Formula
$\operatorname{coprodEquiv} \in \mathrm{LinearEquiv} (\operatorname{id} (S), \mathrm{Prod} (\mathrm{ContinuousLinearMap} (\operatorname{id} (R), x , M), \mathrm{ContinuousLinearMap} (\operatorname{id} (R), x_{1} , M)), \mathrm{ContinuousLinearMap} (\operatorname{id} (R), \mathrm{Prod} (x , x_{1}), M))$
French
L'équivalence de coproduct est une équivalence linéaire de $\mathrm{Prod}$ appliqué à l'ensemble d'applications linéaires continues de $x$ et $M$ et l'ensemble d'applications linéaires continues de $x_{1}$ et $M$ et l'ensemble d'applications linéaires continues de $\mathrm{Prod}$ appliqué à $x$ et $x_{1}$ et $M$.

ContinuousLinearMap.inldefMathlib.Topology.Algebra.Module.ContinuousLinearMap.PiProd, function or constant
Wording
the left injection of x1 and x5 (from the name)
English
Let $R$, $x_{1}$ and $x_{5}$ be sets. Then the left injection of $x_{1}$ and $x_{5}$ is a continuous linear map of $x_{1}$ and $\mathrm{Prod}$ applied to $x_{1}$ and $x_{5}$ defined as the product of the identity map of $R$ and $x_{1}$ and $0$.

$R , x_{1} , x_{5} \in \mathrm{Set} \vdash \operatorname{inl} (R , x_{1} , x_{5}):= \operatorname{prod} (\operatorname{id} (R , x_{1}), 0)\in \mathrm{ContinuousLinearMap} (\operatorname{id} (R), x_{1} , \mathrm{Prod} (x_{1} , x_{5}))$

ContinuousLinearMap.inrdefMathlib.Topology.Algebra.Module.ContinuousLinearMap.PiProd, function or constant
Wording
the right injection of x1 and x5 (from the name)
English
Let $R$, $x_{1}$ and $x_{5}$ be sets. Then the right injection of $x_{1}$ and $x_{5}$ is a continuous linear map of $x_{5}$ and $\mathrm{Prod}$ applied to $x_{1}$ and $x_{5}$ defined as the product of $0$ and the identity map of $R$ and $x_{5}$.
Formula

$f \in \mathrm{LinearPMap} (\operatorname{id} (R), E , F)\vdash \operatorname{closure} (f):= \mathrm{dite} (\operatorname{IsClosable} (f), \lambda \mathrm{hf} . \operatorname{choose} (\mathrm{hf}), \lambda \mathrm{hf1} . f)\in \mathrm{LinearPMap} (\operatorname{id} (R), E , F)$
French
Soit $f$ une application linéaire de $E$ et $F$. Alors la clôture de $f$ est une application linéaire de $E$ et $F$ défini comme $\mathrm{dite}$ appliqué à $\operatorname{IsClosable}$ appliqué à $f$, la fonction qui envoie $\mathrm{hf}$ à $\operatorname{choose}$ appliqué à $\mathrm{hf}$ et la fonction qui envoie $\mathrm{hf1}$ à $f$.

Mathlib.Topology.Algebra.PontryaginDual

PontryaginDualdefMathlib.Topology.Algebra.PontryaginDual, type

no wording

PontryaginDual.mapdefMathlib.Topology.Algebra.PontryaginDual, function or constant
Wording
the map of f (from the name)
English
Let $f$ be a continuous monoid homomorphism of $A$ and $B$. Then the map of $f$ is a continuous monoid homomorphism of $\mathrm{PontryaginDual}$ applied to $B$ and $\mathrm{PontryaginDual}$ applied to $A$ defined as the left composition of the set of circles and $f$.
Formula
$f \in \mathrm{ContinuousMonoidHom} (A , B)\vdash \operatorname{map} (f):= \operatorname{compLeft} (\mathrm{Circle} , f)\in \mathrm{ContinuousMonoidHom} (\mathrm{PontryaginDual} (B), \mathrm{PontryaginDual} (A))$
French

Let $A$ and $G$ be sets. Then the map homomorphism of $A$ and $G$ is a continuous monoid homomorphism of the set of continuous monoid homomorphisms of $A$ and $G$ and the set of continuous monoid homomorphisms of $\mathrm{PontryaginDual}$ applied to $G$ and $\mathrm{PontryaginDual}$ applied to $A$.

French

restricted product of A and i (from the name)

Let $x_{14}$ be a function from elements $j_{3}$ of $x_{1}$ to ring homomorphisms of $x_{2}$ applied to $f$ applied to $j_{3}$ and $x_{3}$ applied to $j_{3}$ such that $\operatorname{Eventually}$ holds for the function that maps $j_{2}$ to $\operatorname{MapsTo}$ applied to $\operatorname{coe}$ applied to $x_{14}$ applied to $j_{2}$, $x_{10}$ applied to $f$ applied to $j_{2}$ and $x_{11}$ applied to $j_{2}$ and $x_{5}$. Then $\operatorname{mapAlongRingHom}$ applied to $x_{2}$, $x_{3}$, $f$, $\mathrm{hf}$, $j_{3}$, $x_{14}$ and $h_{2}$ is a ring homomorphism of the set of restricted products of the function that maps $i_{1}$ to $x_{10}$ applied to $i_{1}$ and $x_{4}$ and the set of restricted products of the function that maps $j_{1}$ to $x_{11}$ applied to $j_{1}$ and $x_{5}$.
Formula
$x_{2} \in x \rightarrow \mathrm{Set} , x_{3} \in x_{1} \rightarrow \mathrm{Set} , f \in x_{1} \rightarrow x , \operatorname{Tendsto} (f , x_{5} , x_{4}), x_{14} \in x_{1} \rightarrow \mathrm{RingHom} (x_{2} (f (j_{3})), x_{3} (j_{3})), \operatorname{Eventually} (\lambda j_{2} . \operatorname{MapsTo} (\operatorname{coe} (x_{14} (j_{2})), x_{10} (f (j_{2})), x_{11} (j_{2})), x_{5})\vdash \operatorname{mapAlongRingHom} (x_{2} , x_{3} , f , \mathrm{hf} , j_{3} , x_{14} , h_{2})\in \mathrm{RingHom} (\mathrm{RestrictedProduct} (\lambda i . x_{2} (i), \lambda i_{1} . x_{10} (i_{1}), x_{4}), \mathrm{RestrictedProduct} (\lambda j . x_{3} (j), \lambda j_{1} . x_{11} (j_{1}), x_{5}))$
French

Soit $f$ une fonction des éléments de $x_{1}$ à des éléments de $x$ telle que $\operatorname{Tendsto}$ tienne pour $f$, $x_{5}$ et $x_{4}$. Soit $v$ une valuation de $R$ et $x$. Alors la valuation de $v$ est une valuation de $\mathrm{WithVal}$ appliqué à $v$ et $x$ défini comme la préimage de l'homomorphisme d'anneaux de l'équivalence de $v$ et $v$.

TopologicalSpace.IsSeparabledefMathlib.Topology.Bases, predicate
Wording
s is separable (from the name)
English
Let $s$ be an element of $\wp x$. Then $s$ is separable, if there exists an element $c$ of $\wp x$, such that $c$ is countable and $s$ is less than or equal to the closure of $c$.

Mathlib.Topology.Bornology.Absorbs

AbsorbentdefMathlib.Topology.Bornology.Absorbs, predicate
Wording
M is absorbent (from the docstring)
English
Let $M$ be a set. Let $s$ be an element of $\wp x$. Then $M$ is absorbent, if for all elements $x_{3}$ of $x$, $\mathrm{Absorbs}$ holds for $M$, $s$ and $\operatorname{singleton}$ applied to $x_{3}$.
Formula
$M \in \mathrm{Set} , s \in \wp x \vdash \mathrm{Absorbent} (M , s):\Leftrightarrow \forall x_{3} \in x . \mathrm{Absorbs} (M , s , \operatorname{singleton} (x_{3}))$
French
Soit $M$ un ensemble. Soit $s$ un élément de $\wp x$. Alors $M$ est absorbant si pour tous les éléments $x_{3}$ de $x$, $\mathrm{Absorbs}$ tient pour $M$, $s$ et $\operatorname{singleton}$ appliqué à $x_{3}$.

Bornology.IsBoundeddefMathlib.Topology.Bornology.Basic, predicate
Wording
s is bounded (from the name)
English
Let $s$ be an element of $\wp x$. Then $s$ is bounded, if the complement of $s$ is cobounded.
Formula
$s \in \wp x \vdash \operatorname{IsBounded} (s):\Leftrightarrow \operatorname{IsCobounded} (\operatorname{compl} (s))$
French
Soit $s$ un élément de $\wp x$. Alors $s$ est borné si le complément de $s$ est coborné.
